# Stephan Burian Resigns from Position
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.1
	title = ww1_austriahungary.1.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.1.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = AUS
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				HUN = { country_event = ww1_austriahungary.1 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.1.a
		trigger = {
			original_tag = AUS
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AUS_stephan_burian = {
					is_political_advisor = yes
				}
			}
			deactivate_advisor = AUS_stephan_burian
			activate_advisor = AUH_leon_von_bilinski
		}
		AUS_stephan_burian = {
			set_character_flag = stephan_burian_break
			clr_character_flag = joint_minister_finance_AUS
		}
		AUH_leon_von_bilinski = {
			set_character_flag = can_be_hired
			set_character_flag = joint_minister_finance_AUS
		}
		AUS_wenzel_von_zalenski = {
			set_character_flag = can_be_hired
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = available_political_advisor
		show_ideas_tooltip = AUH_leon_von_bilinski
		custom_effect_tooltip = available_political_advisor
		show_ideas_tooltip = AUS_wenzel_von_zalenski
		set_global_flag = BOS_can_send_memorandum
		BOS = {
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_bosnia.8
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.1.a
		trigger = {
			original_tag = HUN
		}
	}
}

#Stephan Burian appointed Minister besides the King
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.2
	title = ww1_austriahungary.2.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.2.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		AUS_HUN_exists = yes
		original_tag = AUS
		has_government = neutrality
		date > 1913.06.01
		#has_character = HUN_istvan_burian_von_rajecz
		NOT = {
			HUN = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = HUN_istvan_burian_von_rajecz
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.2.a
		remove_trait = {
			character = AUS_stephan_burian
			trait = AUS_gemeinsames_finanzministerium
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_trait = {
				character = AUS_stephan_burian
				trait = minister_besides_king
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			AUS_stephan_burian = {
				clr_character_flag = stephan_burian_break
				remove_advisor_role = {
					slot = political_advisor
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = appointed_minister_besides_king
		}
	}
}

#Trade Convention with Romania
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.3
	title = ww1_austriahungary.3.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.3.a
		FROM = {
			country_event = ww1_austriahungary.4
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			AUS = {
				add_ideas = AUS_romanian_trade_deal
			}
			HUN = {
				add_ideas = AUS_romanian_trade_deal
			}
			ROM = {
				add_ideas = AUS_romanian_trade_deal2
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = AUS
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = -10
				has_war_with = AUS
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.3.b
		FROM = {
			country_event = ww1_austriahungary.5
		}
		AUS = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = ROM
					modifier = small_decrease
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_war = no
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.12
	title = ww1_austriahungary.12.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.12.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

		name = ww1_austriahungary.12.a
		add_stability = -0.025
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_modify_eco.tt
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_EP_consumer_goods = 0.1
			tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				original_tag = AUS
				AUS_HUN_exists = yes
			}
			HUN = {
				country_event = ww1_austriahungary.12
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.14
	title = ww1_austriahungary.14.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.14.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_active_mission = AUS_imperial_elections
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.14.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				TAG = AUS
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_modify_eco.tt
			add_to_variable = {
				AUS_EP_consumer_goods = 0.07
				tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_cons_election_bad_1
			add_to_variable = {
				AUS_conservative_election_performance = -1
			}
			HUN = {
				country_event = ww1_austriahungary.14
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				TAG = HUN
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = HUN_modify_eco_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				HUN_eco_consumer_good = 0.02
				tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.18
	title = ww1_austriahungary.18.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.18.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.18.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_modify_aps.tt
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_aps_stab = -0.03
			tooltip = stability_factor_tt
		}
		add_political_power = -20
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_ruthenian_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_ruthenians_tt
		}
		#Polish
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_pole_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_pole_tt
		}
		#Czechoslovak
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_czechoslovak_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_czechoslovakia_tt
		}
		#Serb
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_serb_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_serb_tt
		}
		#Croats
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_croat_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_croatian_tt
		}
		#Romanian
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_romanian_loyalty = -5
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_romanians_tt
		}
		AUS_update_state_of_realm = yes
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = AUS_rising_price_of_meat
			days = 180
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_modify_eco.tt
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_EP_consumer_goods = 0.05
			tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.23
	title = ww1_austriahungary.23.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.23.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = paul_term_finished
			}
			set_global_flag = AUS_stop_deactivating_paul
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.23.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = AUS_karl_minister
			}
			has_global_flag = second_round_of_choosing_MP_AUS
		}
		#custom_effect_tooltip = historical_tt
		AUS_fire_minister_advisor = yes
		set_global_flag = AUS_karl_minister
		AUH_karl_von_sturgkh = {
			set_character_flag = minister_AUS
		}
		add_ideas = AUH_karl_von_sturgkh_idea
		#activate_advisor = AUH_karl_von_sturgkh
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_global_flag = AUS_paul_minister
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.23.e
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = AUS_paul_minister
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_global_flag = AUS_paul_minister
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = historical_tt
		}
		AUS_fire_minister_advisor = yes
		set_global_flag = AUS_paul_minister
		AUH_paul_gautsch = {
			set_character_flag = minister_AUS
		}
		add_ideas = AUH_paul_gautsch_idea
		#activate_advisor = AUH_paul_gautsch
		set_can_be_fired_in_advisor_role = {
			character = AUH_paul_gautsch
			value = no
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					date < 1911.8.8
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = {
						id = ww1_austriahungary.28
						days = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = AUS_paul_minister
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

				id = ww1_austriahungary.134
				days = 2
			}
		}
	}

			NOT = {

		}
		tag = AUS

	fire_only_once = yes

			AUS_EP_stab = 0.1

			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = AUS_adress_inequality
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				AUS_EP_consumer_goods = -0.1
				tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
			}
		}

			AUS_ruthenian_loyalty = 10
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_ruthenians_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_pole_loyalty = 10
			tooltip = AUS_loyalty_pole_tt
		}
		AUS_update_state_of_realm = yes
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				AUS_is_GAL_state = yes
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = AUS
			}
			add_manpower = 2000
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		}
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				AUS_is_GAL_state = yes
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = AUS
			}
			add_manpower = 2000
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		}
		random_owned_state = {

	id = ww1_austriahungary.271

	desc = ww1_austriahungary.271.d

				set_autonomy = {
					target = HUN

			}
			AUS = {

	title = ww1_austriahungary.281.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.281.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

		name = ww1_austriahungary.281.a
		AUS = {
			puppet = DON
			country_event = ww1_austriahungary.282
		}
		DON = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					AUS = {
						NOT = {
							has_war_with = SOV
						}
					}
				}
				every_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							tag = SOV
							is_ally_with = SOV
						}
						NOT = {
							original_tag = DON
						}
					}
					white_peace = DON
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.281.b
		add_political_power = 10
		country_event = ww1_austriahungary.283
	}
}

#reorganise serbia focus
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary.286
	title = ww1_austriahungary.286.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary.286.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary.286.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = AUS_trialism
						has_completed_focus = AUS_usa_of_austria
					}
				}
			}
			release_puppet = MGS
			set_autonomy = {
				target = MGS
				autonomy_state = autonomy_satellite
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = AUS_trialism				
			}
			CRO = {
				annex_country = {
					target = MGS
				}
				annex_country = {
					target = SER
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = AUS_usa_of_austria				
			}
			release_puppet = SER
			set_autonomy = {
				target = SER
				autonomy_state = autonomy_tgwr_personal_union
			}
		}
	}
}

#vienna milfactory vienna dispatch
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austria_quests.3
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	#fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					4 = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
				}
				4 = {
					state_event = {
						id = ww1_austria_quests.4
						trigger_for = owner
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#mil factory gives quest
state_event = {
	id = ww1_austria_quests.4
	title = ww1_austria_quests.4.t
	desc = ww1_austria_quests.4.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = {
		name = ww1_austria_quests.4.a
		4 = {
			OWNER = {
				unlock_decision_tooltip = AUS_improve_vienna_armament
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = no
			}
			set_state_flag = AUS_built_military_factory
			set_variable = {
				arms_factory_goal = arms_factory_level
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				arms_factory_goal = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

#restore economic confidence
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austria_quests.5
	title = ww1_austria_quests.5.t
	desc = ww1_austria_quests.5.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		set_temp_variable = {
			AUS_stability_temp_var = stability
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			AUS_stability_temp_var = 0.15
		}
		set_variable = {
			AUS_stab_quest = AUS_stability_temp_var
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_stab_quest
			max = 0.55
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austria_quests.5.a
		activate_mission = AUS_restore_economic_confidence
	}
}

		}

		}
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_contain_spdao_democratic_var = -0.045
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_contain_spdao_democratic_var
			min = 0.05
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_contain_spdao_communism_var
			min = 0.02
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austria_quests.7.a
		activate_mission = AUS_contain_the_SPDAO
	}

	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = AUS
		is_in_faction_with = GER
		AUS_ferdinand_von_trauttmansdorff = {
			is_hired_as_advisor = yes
		}
		SER = {
			has_war_with = TUR
		}
		BUL = {
			has_war_with = TUR
		}
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = ROM
		}
		country_exists = ROM
		date > 1912.11.10
		OR = {
			has_subject = HUN
			AUS_has_ROM_state = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			AUS_romanian_quest = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			AUS_romanian_quest = ROM.opinion@AUS
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_romanian_quest = 15
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_romanian_quest
			min = 35
			max = 65
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austria_quests.9.a
		activate_mission = AUS_increase_relations_with_romanians
	}
}

#collapse events
#collapse start
add_namespace = ww1_austriahungary_collapse
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.1
	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.1.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.1.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	trigger = {
		tag = AUS
		OR = {
			check_variable = {
				AUS_total_loyalty < 16
			}
			has_country_flag = AUS_testing_collapse
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			#event would be theoretically on 5 October
			activate_mission = AUS_croat_slovene_serb_council			# 1 day / 6 October
			if = {
				limit = {
					HUN = {
						exists = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.4					#11 days / 16 October
					days = 11
				}
				# triggers the Hungarian event for the Karl speech + option to dismantle Augsleich / # 17 October
				# triggers Aster Revolution event # 23 October
				else = {
					country_event = {
						id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.20						#Makes Hungary independant if they didn't exist/were annexed: 11 days / 16 October
						days = 11
					}
				}
			}
			activate_mission = AUS_bukovina_council			# 12 days / 17 October
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.6				#Steirmark Committee of Welfare - # 15 days / 20 October
				days = 15
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.7				# German-Austria provisional assembly - #16 days - 21 October
				days = 16
			}
			activate_mission = AUS_polish_council			# 23 days / 28 October
			activate_mission = AUS_czech_council			# Release Czechia event - 23 days / 28 October
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.9				#? - 
				days = 24
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.10				#Slavs leave - 24 days / 29 october 1918
				days = 24
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.13				# zakopane creation - 32 days / 30 October
				days = 25
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12				#lviv/lemberg occupied by ukrainian rebels - 27 days / 1 November 
				days = 30
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.14				# komacza creation - 31 days / 4 November
				days = 31
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.15				# tarnobrzeg creation - 33 days / 6 November
				days = 33
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.16				#
				days = 36
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.21				# Lemko Republic creation - 5 December
				days = 61
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.22				# Hutsul Republic creation - 8 January
				days = 95
			}
			#future events
			#karl abdication November 11
			#Temporary Governing Committee of lviv/lwow: 23 November to 10 january as it was merged in the Liquidation commission
			#liquidation commission dissolved 23 March 1919
			#country_event = {
			#	id = ww1_auh.15
			#	days = 45
			#}
			# galicia spawn
		}
	}
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.1.a
		add_stability = -0.05
	}
}

#polish council
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.3
	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.3.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.3.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				GAL = {
					is_subject_of = AUS
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = GAL
					OR = {
						state = 237
						state = 1269
						state = 1268
						state = 1270
						state = 562
						state = 91
						state = 89
					}
					is_core_of = GAL
				}
				transfer_state_to = AUS
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_owned_by = AUS
					is_owned_by = HUN
				}
				OR = {
					state = 88 #Krakow
					state = 237 #Lemkow
					state = 991 #Bielitz
					state = 1269 #Zakopane
					state = 1268 #Tarnobrzeg
					state = 72 #Teschen
					#state = 1270
					#state = 562
					#state = 91
					#state = 89
				}
				#is_core_of = GAL
			}
			transfer_state_to = GAL
		}
		set_global_flag = galicia_released
		set_global_flag = AUS_collapse_spawn_tarnobrzeg
		hidden_effect = {
			#liquidation committee
			if = {
				limit = {
					POL = {
						exists = yes
					}
				}
				POL = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = GAL
						autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
						end_civil_wars = yes
						end_wars = yes
					}
				}
				GAL = {
					clr_country_flag = monarchist_puppet
					set_cosmetic_tag = GAL_liquidation_comitte
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = neutrality
						long_name = GAL_liquidation_comitte_party_long
						name = GAL_liquidation_comitte_party
						elections_allowed = no
					}
					set_popularities = {
						democratic = 46
						fascism = 0
						communism = 8
						neutrality = 46
					}
					POL_wincenty_witos = {
						promote_character = authoritarian_democracy
					}	
				}
			}
			#poland doesnt exist just release
			else = {
				clr_country_flag = monarchist_puppet
				POL_wincenty_witos = { #change? idk
					promote_character = authoritarian_democracy
				}	
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.3.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
			limit = {
				GAL = {
					is_subject_of = AUS
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = GAL
					OR = {
						state = 237
						state = 1269
						state = 1268
						state = 1270
						state = 562
						state = 91
						state = 89
					}
					is_core_of = GAL
				}
				transfer_state_to = AUS
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_owned_by = AUS
					is_owned_by = HUN
				}
				OR = {
					state = 88 #Krakow
					state = 237 #Lemkow
					state = 991 #Bielitz
					state = 1269 #Zakopane
					state = 1268 #Tarnobrzeg
					#state = 1270
					#state = 562
					#state = 91
					#state = 89
				}
				is_core_of = GAL
			}
			transfer_state_to = GAL
		}
		set_global_flag = galicia_released
		set_global_flag = AUS_collapse_spawn_tarnobrzeg
		hidden_effect = {
			#liquidation committee
			if = {
				limit = {
					POL = {
						exists = yes
					}
				}
				POL = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = GAL
						autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
						end_civil_wars = yes
						end_wars = yes
					}
				}
				GAL = {
					clr_country_flag = monarchist_puppet
					set_cosmetic_tag = GAL_liquidation_comitte
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = neutrality
						long_name = GAL_liquidation_comitte_party_long
						name = GAL_liquidation_comitte_party
						elections_allowed = no
					}
					set_popularities = {
						democratic = 46
						fascism = 0
						communism = 8
						neutrality = 46
					}
					POL_wincenty_witos = {
						promote_character = authoritarian_democracy
					}	
				}
			}
			#poland doesnt exist just release
			else = {
				clr_country_flag = monarchist_puppet
				POL_wincenty_witos = { #change? idk
					promote_character = authoritarian_democracy
				}	
			}
		}
		}
	}
}

	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.8.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.8.d

	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = collapse_of_austria_hungary_CZE
		hidden_effect = {
			AUH_release_CZE = yes
			CZE = {
				complete_national_focus = CZE_birth
				promote_character = CZE_karel_kramar
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					state = 9
					is_owned_by = AUS
				}
				transfer_state_to = CZE
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.8.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			AUH_release_CZE = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.8.b
		effect_tooltip = {
			AUH_release_CZE = yes
		}
		CZE = {
			change_tag_from = AUS
		}
	}
}

#south slavs declare independence
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.10
	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.10.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.10.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = collapse_of_austria_hungary_YUG
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					HUN = {
						owns_state = 208
					}
				}
				CRO = {
					transfer_state = 208
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_owned_by = HUN
						is_owned_by = AUS
					}
					state = 109
				}
				CRO = {
					safe_transfer_state_to = yes
				}
			}
			AUH_release_CRO_state_of_croats_and_slovenes = yes
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_owned_by = HUN
						is_owned_by = AUS
						is_owned_by = BOS
					}
					OR = {
						is_core_of = BOS
						is_core_of = CRO
					}
					NOT = {
						state = 163
					}
				}
				CRO = {
					safe_transfer_state_to = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.10.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			AUH_release_CRO_state_of_croats_and_slovenes = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		effect_tooltip = {
			AUH_release_CRO_state_of_croats_and_slovenes = yes
		}
		CRO = {
			change_tag_from = AUS
		}
	}
}

#lemberg occupied by ukrainian rebels
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12
	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = AUS
						#is_owned_and_controlled_by = GAL
					}
					is_core_of = WUK
					AUS_is_UKR_state = yes
				}
				transfer_state_to = WUK
				remove_core_of = ROOT
				remove_claim_by = ROOT
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = AUS
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = GAL
					}
					OR = {
						state = 80
						state = 1007
					}
				}
				transfer_state_to = WUK
				remove_core_of = ROOT
				remove_claim_by = ROOT
			}
			ROM = {
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.25
					days = 15
				}
			}
			WUK = {
				inherit_technology = ROOT
				add_ideas = standing_alone
				set_cosmetic_tag = WUK_ukrainian_state #renamed shortly after
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = UKR
					}
					give_military_access = UKR
					diplomatic_relation = {
						country = UKR
						relation = non_aggression_pact
					}
					UKR = {
						give_military_access = PREV
						diplomatic_relation = {
							country = UKR
							relation = non_aggression_pact
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						num_divisions < 5
					}
					load_oob = "WUK_army"
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.25
					days = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12.a
		hidden_effect = {
			WUK = {
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.23
					days = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.12.b
		WUK = {
			change_tag_from = AUS
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			WUK = {
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.23
					days = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Aster Revolution
country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.19
	title = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.19.t
	desc = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.19.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	#fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		HUN = {
			set_country_flag = collapse_of_austria_hungary_HUN
			hidden_effect = {
				delete_unit = {
					disband = no						#to avoid hungary being too strong when at war with romania
				}
				load_oob = HUN_1918
				#karolyi gov
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_government = democratic
						}
					}
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = democratic
						elections_allowed = yes
					}
					promote_character = HUN_mihaly_karolyi
					set_popularities = {
						democratic = 40
						neutrality = 36
						communism = 24
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = collapse_of_austria_hungary_HUN
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_cosmetic_tag = HUN_archkingdom
					}
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					clr_country_flag = HUN_archkingdom
				}
				###
				retire_character = HUN_istvan_tisza					#killed, add assassination as event
				remove_ideas = AUH_ww1_House_Habsburg_Lorraine
				remove_ideas = HUN_ausgleich
				add_ideas = HUN_catholic_church_opposition
				add_ideas = HUN_disorganized_army
				add_ideas = HUN_socialist_unrest
				#remove dynam modifiers, change later when coding hungary interwar
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = HUN_army }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = HUN_transitioning_economy }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = HUN_croatian_sabor }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = HUN_magyarization_and_minorities }
				#post soviet coup?
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = neutrality
					long_name = HUN_regency_party
					name = HUN_regency_party
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character = AUH_joseph_august_von_osterreich
					}
					promote_character = AUH_joseph_august_von_osterreich
					else = {
						AUS = {
							AUH_joseph_august_von_osterreich = {
								set_nationality = HUN
								set_character_name = HUN_joseph_august
								promote_character = semi_constitutional_monarchy
							}
						}
					}
				}
				mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_owned_by = AUS
						OR = {
							is_core_of = HUN
							is_core_of = TRA
							is_core_of = SLO
							state = 73
						}
						NOT = {
							state = 80
							state = 867
							state = 1007
						}
						is_controlled_by = AUS
					}
					transfer_state_to = HUN
					remove_core_of = AUS
				}
				AUH_franz_rohr_von_denta = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_field_marshal = no
						}
						promote_leader = yes
					}
					set_nationality = HUN
					set_character_name = HUN_franz_rohr_von_denta
				}
				HUN_miklos_horthy = { set_nationality = HUN }
				HUN_gyula_andrassy = { set_nationality = HUN }
				# Banat Republic
				BNT = {
					set_country_flag = great_war_loser
					add_ideas = standing_alone
				}
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 82
							state = 764
						}
					}
					add_core_of = BNT
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_by = HUN
						}
						transfer_state_to = BNT
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					# without this, hungary loses crisana in the midst of revolutionary war
					# I moved this to here because sometimes revolution does not trigger before conference effect
					every_state = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_by = HUN
						}
						set_state_flag = dont_transfer
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.19.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = AUS
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						owns_state = 43
						HUN = {
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				AUH_release_HUN = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					HUN = {
						OR = {
							tag = ROOT
							has_country_flag = great_war_winner
						}
					}
				}
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						state = 27
						is_owned_or_subject_PREV_trigger = yes
					}
					transfer_state_to = HUN
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.19.b
		trigger = {
			tag = HUN
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = HUN
					HUN = {
						has_country_leader = {
							character = HUN_sandor_wekerle
						}
					}
				}
				promote_character = HUN_janos_hadik
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						owns_state = 43
						HUN = {
							is_subject_of = AUS
						}
					}
				}
				AUH_release_HUN = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					HUN = {
						OR = {
							tag = ROOT
							has_country_flag = great_war_winner
						}
					}
				}
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						state = 27
						is_owned_or_subject_PREV_trigger = yes
					}
					transfer_state_to = HUN
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.20
	title = ww1_auh.218.t
	desc = ww1_auh.218.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_karl_i_coronation
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = hungary_abolish_ausgleich
		set_country_flag = hungary_rebel_another_time
		AUS = {
			release = HUN
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state = 70
						state = 71
						state = 664
						state = 1020
						is_core_of = HUN
						is_core_of = ROM
					}
					NOT = {
						state = 147
						state = 80
						state = 1007
					}
				}
				transfer_state_to = HUN
				set_state_flag = dont_transfer
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriahungary_collapse.20.a
		trigger = {
			tag = AUS
		}
		add_political_power = -100
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_hungarian_army_leave
		transfer_units_fraction = {
			target = HUN
			size = 0.35
			target_organization = 1
			source_organization = 1
			navy_ratio = 0
			keep_unit_leaders_trigger = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ww1_german_austria.1
	title = ww1_german_austria.1.t
	desc = ww1_german_austria.1.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ww1_german_austria.1.a
		set_cosmetic_tag = AUR
		# German-Austria
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject = no
				OR = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					has_war_together_with = GER
				}
				GER = {
					is_subject = no
					NOT = {
						has_government = communism
					}
				}
			}
			get_current_government_type = yes
			GER = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = ROOT
					autonomy_state = autonomy_tgwr_unilateral_union
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
			}
			restore_previous_government_type = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUS_entente_might_have_other_plans
		GER = {
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_german_austria.2
				days = 2
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_german_austria.8
				days = 4
			}
		}
	}
}

			AUS_overton_window_quest = AUS_overton_window
		}

		}
		set_variable = {

		}
		add_to_variable = {

			min = 0.05
		}
		set_variable = {

		}
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_contain_spdao_democratic_var = -0.075
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_contain_spdao_democratic_var
			min = 0.05
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			AUS_stability_temp_var = stability
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			AUS_stability_temp_var = 0.11
		}
		set_variable = {
			AUS_stab_quest = AUS_stability_temp_var
		}
		set_variable = {
			AUS_war_support_quest_var = war_support
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			AUS_war_support_quest_var = 0.1
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_war_support_quest_var
			min = 0
			max = 0.99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_austriadebug.2.a
		activate_mission = AUS_restore_economic_confidence
		activate_mission = AUS_push_the_public_against_democracy
		activate_mission = AUS_contain_the_SPDAO
		activate_mission = AUS_increase_public_perception_of_war
		activate_mission = AUS_improve_vienna_armament
	}
}

#MGM
country_event = {
	id = ww1_occupiedmontenegro.1
	title = ww1_occupiedmontenegro.1.t
	desc = ww1_occupiedmontenegro.1.d
	#picture = GFX_report_event_gathering_protest
	fire_only_once = yes
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}
	trigger = {
		is_great_war_ongoing = yes
		original_tag = AUS
		has_war_with = MNT
		105 = {
			is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes
		}
		761 = {
			is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes
		}
		848 = {
			is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ww1_occupiedmontenegro.1.a
		create_dynamic_country = {
			original_tag = MNT
			set_country_flag = AUH_mnt_govnerate_flag
			set_cosmetic_tag = MGM_fascism
			set_research_slots = 2
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = neutrality
				long_name = GUD_neutrality_party
				name = GUD_neutrality_party
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			reserve_dynamic_country = yes
			set_capital = {
				state = 105
			}
		}
		105 = {
			transfer_state_to = MGM
		}
		MGM = {
			set_state_controller = 1205
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						TUR = {
							owns_state = 761
							owns_state = 848
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_controller = 761
				set_state_controller = 848
			}
		}
		add_to_faction = MGM
		puppet = MGM
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space_tt
	}
}
